Assemble only from `assets/crafted/` (never raw `assets/gen/` videos)\n\n## File counts\n\n- Top-level docs: 2 (`README.md`, `IDE-HANDOFF.md`)\n- `docs/`: 9\n- `manifests/`: 3\n- `assets/gen/`: 18\n- `assets/crafted/`: 4\n- **Total: 36 files** (~35MB). CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\nTurns the manual Kaatrukulle lane into a one-click **Template 1** job inside `/assembly`. Stops at preview. **No Publish, no `main` push, no Supabase apply.**\n\nStacked on PR #26 (RH12 Kaatrukulle) so RH12 stays reserved; iteration 2 allocates `royal-heritage-13`.\n\n## What it does\n`/assembly` → \"Template 1\" card: pin URL, display name, couple names, parent (RH7), music-library track, budget ($4) → **Run**.\n\n1. **pin** — resolve pin → `pin-ref.jpg`; sample palette (primary / secondary / paper) for the theme (\"font color\").\n2. **gen** — parallel Replicate `xai/grok-imagine-image` stills (FIRST, LAST + no-text QA w/ one regen, plate1, plate2), then hero still → Replicate hero video 6s **in parallel with** xAI opening 12s (`image`=FIRST, `last_frame`=LAST). Budget gate before every call. **First moderated video stops the job** (no auto-retry).\n3. **craft** — mute `-an`, +3s last-frame hold, plates → `{id}-section-1..5.jpg`, music copied from `cms/music-library.json` (never muxed). ffprobe 0 audio streams or fail closed.\n4. **assemble** — dry-run then real `assemblePremium`, theme CSS block, `previewDefaults`, `music`/`musicName`, `musicTracks`, editor option.\n5. **preview** — demo link + spend line.\n\nPrompts default to the **wire-proven Kaatrukulle strings** from PR #27 packs (`server/assembly-template1-prompts.mjs`, bit-identical test).\n\n## Files\n- `server/assembly-template1-prompts.mjs`, `-gen.mjs`, `-craft.mjs`, `-theme.mjs`, `assembly-template1.mjs`, `music-library.mjs`\n- `api/assembly.mjs`: `template1-start|status|cancel`, `music-library`\n- `src/Assembly.tsx`: Template 1 card (progress, spend, palette swatches, cancel, preview link)\n- `cms/music-library.json` (Vazhithunaiye seed), `scripts/assemble-template1.mjs` headless twin\n- `server/assembly.mjs`: `// reserved:` line parsing for held slots (fixes the `server-journey` `id:'` regex clash from #26)\n- `server/assembly-ai.mjs`: `runXaiImagineVideo` accepts `image`, returns cost ticks / `respectModeration`\n- Tests: `tests/server-assembly-template1.mjs` (recorded payloads, no network); `npm run test:server` 171/171 locally\n\n## Proof run\nMocked end-to-end (fake Replicate/xAI serving iteration-1 bytes) on a temp repo copy: RH13 allocated, 20 files written, both mp4s 0 audio streams, spend ledger $2.28. catch-all and catch-all `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` exclude both paths\n- `Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8` on both paths\n- Share-card `/:slug` uses the same guest-slug regex as rewrite, headers, reserved slugs, guest-page order). CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\n## Problem\n\nLive `https://findmyinvite.com/blog` and `/blog/{slug}` returned HTTP 200 with the homepage SPA title (`Create Invitation Webpage Online for All Events | FindMyInvite`). Soft-404 for crawlers. Cause: `/blog` matched the guest `/:slug` rewrite (reserved → SPA shell) and `/blog/{slug}` fell through to `index.html`.\n\n## Fix\n\nSame pattern as `/invitations/{slug}`: Vercel rewrites → serverless HTML handler.\n\n- `/blog` and `/blog/:slug` rewrites sit **before** the guest `/:slug` gate so the journal is not swallowed.\n- SPA catch-all also excludes `blog(?:/.*)?`.\n- Unknown slugs return HTTP 404 HTML (`text/html`), not 200 SPA.\n- Canonicals and sitemap use `https://findmyinvite.com` only (no `vercel.app`).\n\nHandler verification (local, same as post-deploy curl):\n\n| URL | Status | Title / H1 |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `/blog` | 200 | The FindMyInvite journal |\n| `/blog/halloween-invitation-webpage` | 200 | Halloween Invitation Webpage for Parties and Events |\n| `/blog/this-should-404` | 404 | Article not found |\n\nNone of those pages include the homepage title.\n\n## Files touched\n\n- `vercel.json` — blog rewrites, SPA exclusion, `includeFiles` for `content/blog/**`\n- `api/blog-page.mjs` + `server/blog-page.mjs` — SSR HTML (title, description, canonical, H1, body)\n- `server/blog-posts.mjs` — load `content/blog/*.md` (and `*.json`) + merge published Supabase `blog_posts`; files win on slug conflict\n- `api/content.mjs` — `/api/content?kind=blog` uses the same store (SPA journal stays in sync)\n- `server/local-api.mjs` — local `/blog` routing\n- `content/blog/halloween-invitation-webpage.md` — seed Halloween invite-angle draft (`seed: true` in frontmatter only)\n- `public/sitemap.xml` — keep `/blog`, add seed post URL\n- `src/App.tsx` — do not overwrite SSR blog titles after client navigation\n- `tests/server-blog-page.mjs` + rewrite/sitemap assertions in invitation/guest/share tests\n- `documents/post-handoff-changelog.md` — publish recipe\n\nGuest `/:slug`, invitation LPs, studio, `robots.txt`, and static `sitemap.xml` serving are unchanged.\n\n## How Content / SEO Lead adds daily Halloween posts through 19 Oct\n\n1. Add `content/blog/{kebab-slug}.md` with frontmatter:\n   - `slug` (lowercase kebab)\n   - `title` (include ` \\| FindMyInvite`)\n   - `h1`\n   - `description` (meta)\n   - `excerpt` (index card)\n   - `published: true`\n   - `published_at` ISO datetime **already reached** (future dates stay hidden)\n   - Body: plaintext paragraphs separated by a blank line — not raw HTML\n2. Add `<loc>https://findmyinvite.com/blog/{kebab-slug}</loc>` to `public/sitemap.xml`.\n3. Merge to `main` (Vercel Git deploys production). Curl `/blog/{slug}` and confirm unique title/H1, `Content-Type: text/html`, and no homepage title.\n4. Optional: also insert the same row into prod `blog_posts`. Not required — files are enough for SSR and `/api/content?kind=blog`.\n\nSeed URL: `https://findmyinvite.com/blog/halloween-invitation-webpage`\n\n## After merge (do not merge from this `7e5d4c7`) had no `/studio` route and no `api/studio.mjs`. `/invitations/{slug}`.\n\nEach design URL now returns unique 200 HTML with meta title, description, H1, canonical, primary CTA, template-switch copy, related links, and FAQ copy from the P1 brief. Unknown `/invitations/{slug}` still returns HTTP 404 with `x-fmi-invitation-page: 404`. Guest gate `/api/guest-page` and `robots.txt` Disallow `/akay` are unchanged.\n\n## CTA URL choice\nDesign primary CTAs use the existing editor pattern already used by Gallery **Use This Design** and the live-demo **use this design** button:\n\n`/create?template={template_catalog id}&type=wedding`\n\nThat lands the host on the create form with the live catalogue row pre-selected. Occasion pages keep `/templates?collection={tier}&type=wedding`. Design “View live demo” uses `/invite/demo?template={id}&type=wedding` for that same SKU.\n\nSKU ids follow the brief match notes (display name ≠ catalog id in four cases):\n\n| Landing slug | CTA `template_catalog` id |\n| --- | --- |\n| `/invitations/royal-imperial` | `rose-gold-blush-royal` |\n| `/invitations/royal-elegance` | `modern-minimal-royal` |\n| `/invitations/crimson-royale` | `ivory-elegance` |\n| `/invitations/majestic-love` | `royal-elegance` |\n\nThe other nine slugs match their catalog ids. Royal Temple and competitor SKU names are not added.\n\n## Approach\n- New `server/design-landings.mjs` merged into `occasionBySlug` in `server/occasion-landings.mjs` so server HTML and SPA `OccasionLanding` share one lookup.\n- `ctaHref(page)` uses `templateId` when present, otherwise the occasion collection browse link.\n- `public/sitemap.xml` lists all 13 new `/invitations/...` URLs (34 invitation URLs total).\n\n## Tests\n- Known design slug → 200 HTML with unique title/H1 and catalog-id CTA.\n- Unknown slug → 404 not-found HTML.\n- Guest-page rewrite and apex `/:slug` soft-404 CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\n## Why\nEpic 2 gap-fill for live `/akay/shortlist`. The list, filters, chips, cards, and drawer already exist. Approve on `main` still PATCHes `shortlist_candidates` then POSTs `replication_queue` as two REST writes, so it cannot meet “same transaction” and live queue stayed at **0**.\n\n## What\n- **G1** Approve a `proposed` card → `approved` + exactly one `replication_queue` row (`queued`, empty assignee) via privileged RPC `approve_shortlist_candidate`, idempotent on `candidate_id`. Re-approve → 409, queue count unchanged.\n- **G2** Reject a `proposed` card → `rejected`; never touches `replication_queue` (`reject_shortlist_candidate`).\n- **G3** Optional bulk Approve/Reject for selected proposed rows (max 50, per-id ok/fail). Primary path stays one-by-one on the card and drawer.\n- Card preview is cropped so Approve/Reject stay visible; non-proposed actions/checkboxes stay disabled.\n- Does **not** recreate list/filters, competitor approvals, Scout mining, or Pipeline/Upcoming promote.\n\nRPCs are already on prod Supabase `qqvcptjkfcjkwbkookcm` (`shortlist_decide_rpcs`, `service_role` execute only). This PR is the operator API + small card-visibility glue. SQL is recorded in `supabase/003_shortlist_decide.sql`.\n\n## Verification\n- `tests/server-akay-shortlist.mjs`: approve enqueues once through RPC; re-approve 409; reject never hits `replication_queue`; bulk >50 is 400.\n- RPC smoke on prod (missing id): `{ok:false,error:not_found}` with no writes.\n- No `middleware.js` / `proxy.matcher`.\n\n## QA (after merge)\n1. Open `/akay/shortlist` (default Proposed).\n2. Approve **one** proposed card → Approved +1 and `replication_queue` 0→1 (`queued`).\n3. Approve the same id again → error; queue still 1.\n4. Reject another proposed card → Rejected +1; queue unchanged.\n5. Optional: select a few proposed → bulk Approve.\n\nDo not treat the 3 already-rejected video templates as this QA. Live baseline at this pass: 22 proposed / 3 rejected / 0 approved / queue 0.\n\nSupersedes stale #7 (same RPC intent, older `main`). Merge to `main` is the production UI/API release.\n<!-- CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_END -->\n\n<div><a CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\n## Why PR #9 did not fix production\nMerged middleware + `proxy.entrypoint` is on `main`, but live curl still returns the SPA:\n\n```\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/wedding-invitation-classic-7\n# HTTP/2 200\n# content-disposition: inline; filename=\"index.html\"\n# etag: 061f7660691eabcc2d0b4063a44661d8\n# x-vercel-cache: HIT\n```\n\nThat is the static `index.html` object, not a 404 document. Two bugs in #9 could produce this even before CDN:\n\n1. **Middleware never matched / never ran** on this Vite project. The matcher was a long negative-lookahead regex; production curl has **no** gate header (there was none to send). `x-vercel-cache: HIT` of `index.html` is what you get when the request never left the SPA rewrite.\n2. **Fail-open was too wide.** `invitation()` → `db()` required `configured()`, which needs `RATE_LIMIT_SECRET` even for a public read. Any non-404/400 throw (missing env in the middleware runtime, 503, network) returned `false` and continued to the SPA **for every unknown slug**.\n\nCDN HIT of the old `index.html` is a symptom, not the root cause. Middleware must 404 before that file is served; this PR does not rely on it.\n\n## What this PR does\n**Primary (reliable):** rewrite slug-shaped paths to `/api/guest-page`, the same Node serverless runtime that already serves `/api/invitations` in production (Supabase env is proven there).\n\n| Path | Result | `x-fmi-guest-gate` |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Dead / unpublished / expired guest slug | HTTP **404** HTML | `404` |\n| Published unexpired invite (`/ashoksupriya`) | HTTP **200** SPA | `pass` |\n| Reserved storefront (`/templates`, `/about`, …) | HTTP **200** SPA | `skip` |\n| Transient DB/network error | HTTP **200** SPA (fail-open) | `open` |\n\n**Also:**\n- Matcher simplified to `/:slug` (no lookahead that Vercel might ignore).\n- DB reads use `databaseConfigured()` (`SUPABASE_URL` + service role only). Guest 404 no longer depends on `RATE_LIMIT_SECRET`.\n- 404 and pass responses send `x-fmi-guest-gate` so curl can prove the gate ran. If that header is missing, the rewrite/middleware still did not execute.\n- `dist/index.html` is included in the function so live invites can be served without looping the SPA catch-all.\n\nOut of scope: occasion LPs, sitemap growth.\n\n## Verification (preview first)\nWait until the Vercel **Preview** for this branch is Ready. Then:\n\n```bash\n# Dead slug — must be 404 HTML, not index.html\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview>/wedding-invitation-classic-7\"\n# HTTP/2 404\n# x-fmi-guest-gate: 404\n# content-type: text/html\n# must NOT be content-disposition: filename=\"index.html\"\n\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview>/zzzz-not-a-real-invite-999\"\n# HTTP/2 404\n# x-fmi-guest-gate: 404\n\n# Published invite\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview>/ashoksupriya\"\n# HTTP/2 200\n# x-fmi-guest-gate: pass\n\n# Storefront\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview>/templates\"\n# HTTP/2 200\n# x-fmi-guest-gate: skip\n```\n\nIf preview 404s are correct and `x-fmi-guest-gate` is present, the gate ran. Merge to `main` (Vercel Git integration = production).\n\n## Production after merge\nDo not treat a CDN HIT of the old `index.html` etag as success. Confirm:\n\n```bash\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/wedding-invitation-classic-7\n# 404 + x-fmi-guest-gate: 404\n\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/ashoksupriya\n# 200 + x-fmi-guest-gate: pass\n\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/templates\n# 200 + x-fmi-guest-gate: skip\n```\n\nIf production is 200 **without** `x-fmi-guest-gate`, the rewrite still is not applying — report that; do not blame cache alone.\n\n## Tests\n`npm run test:server` — 43/43 pass.\n\n<!-- CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_END -->\n\n<div><a CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\n## Problem\nGuest URLs matching `/^\\/[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,47}$/` are handled by a client catch-all (`PublicInvitation`). Vercel rewrites those paths to `index.html`, so dead/old template SEO slugs such as `/wedding-invitation-classic-*` return **HTTP 200** SPA HTML (soft-404). Google Search Console reports ~870 Not found (404) while live curl often sees 200.\n\nClient-only React cannot set the HTTP status.\n\n## Change\nAdd Vercel Node routing middleware (`middleware.js`, `runtime: 'nodejs'`) that looks up the slug with the same public-read rules as `/api/invitations?action=read`:\n\n- **Missing, unpublished, or expired** → real **HTTP 404** HTML (link to `https://findmyinvite.com/templates`)\n- **Published and unexpired** → continue to the existing SPA rewrite (guest page still 200)\n- **Reserved storefront paths** (`/templates`, `/create`, `/dashboard`, `/login`, `/signup`, `/about`, `/blog`, `/akay`, `/grand-launch`, `/manage/*`, `/invite/*`, `/api/*`, `/assets/*`, …) are never treated as guest slugs\n- **DB/config errors fail open** so a Supabase blip does not 404 real guest links\n\nThe same gate is wired into the local Vite adapter (`server/local-api.mjs`) so `npm run dev` / preview match production.\n\n`vercel.json` sets `proxy.entrypoint` to `middleware.js` so this Vite SPA actually runs Routing Middleware.\n\nOut of scope (unchanged): occasion landing pages, sitemap growth, WhatsApp share-card fallback for `/api/share?slug=`.\n\n## Verification\nAfter the Vercel **Preview** for this branch is Ready (do not wait for production; this branch is not `main`):\n\n```bash\n# Dead slug — must be 404, not 200 SPA\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview-host>/wedding-invitation-classic-does-not-exist-xyz\"\n# expect: HTTP/2 404\n# expect: content-type: text/html\n# body should say \"Invitation not found\" and must not be the SPA index.html shell\n\n# Reserved storefront — still 200\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview-host>/templates\"\n# expect: HTTP/2 200\n\n# Published guest invite (prod currently has public slug ashoksupriya; preview uses the same Supabase)\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview-host>/ashoksupriya\"\n# expect: HTTP/2 200\ncurl -sI \"https://<preview-host>/test-couple\"\n# 404 if that fixture is not in prod; 200 only if a published unexpired row exists\n```\n\nToday on production (before this lands):\n\n```\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/wedding-invitation-classic-does-not-exist-xyz\n# HTTP/2 200  (soft-404 — this PR’s bug)\n\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/ashoksupriya\n# HTTP/2 200  (keep this)\n```\n\nLocal: `npm run test:server` (includes `tests/server-guest-page.mjs`).\n\n## Tests\nAll 41 `npm run test:server` tests pass, including new cases for reserved paths, missing/unpublished/expired → 404, published → continue, and fail-open on config/DB errors.\n\n<!-- CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\nProduction currently serves the SPA `index.html` shell for `/robots.txt` and `/sitemap.xml` (verified live 2026-09-17: HTTP 200, `content-type: text/html`, `content-disposition: filename=index.html`). This PR ships the actual crawl files from Vite `public/` and stops the Vercel SPA catch-all rewrite from swallowing them.\n\n## What changed\n\n- Added `public/robots.txt` (v1): allow `/`, disallow `/akay` and `/akay/`, point Sitemap at `https://findmyinvite.com/sitemap.xml`.\n- Added `public/sitemap.xml` with only public indexable apex URLs that already exist in `src/App.tsx` (and match the existing `X-Robots-Tag` allowlist in `vercel.json`).\n- Updated `vercel.json`:\n  - SPA rewrite negative lookahead now also excludes `robots.txt` and `sitemap.xml` (same pattern as `api/`, `assets/`, `review/`).\n  - Explicit `Content-Type` headers: `text/plain` for robots, `application/xml` for the sitemap.\n  - Those two paths are also excluded from the blanket `noindex` header so they are not tagged as HTML pages.\n- Extended `tests/server-share.mjs` so the SPA rewrite regex does **not** match `/robots.txt` or `/sitemap.xml`, and so the file bodies start with `User-agent:` / XML `urlset`.\n\n## Sitemap URLs (and why)\n\nOnly routes that exist in `App.tsx` and are public marketing/legal pages (footer nav + template listing). No invented paths. No `*.vercel.app`. No `/akay`, `/create`, `/dashboard`, `/login`, `/signup`, `/forgot-password`, `/manage/*`, `/invite/*`, or guest invitation slugs.\n\n| URL | Why it is included |\n| --- | --- |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/` | Homepage (`path=== '/'` → `Home`) |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/templates` | Template listing (`Gallery`) |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/about` | Marketing page (`ContentPages`) |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/contact` | Marketing page (`ContentPages`) |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/blog` | Journal listing (`ContentPages`); individual `/blog/:slug` posts are CMS-driven and were not invented |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/terms` | Legal page (`ContentPages` + footer) |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/privacy-policy` | Legal page (`ContentPages` + footer) |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/refund-policy` | Legal page (`ContentPages` + footer) |\n| `https://findmyinvite.com/shipping-policy` | Legal page (`ContentPages` + footer) |\n\n## How to verify (after this is on production `main`)\n\n```bash\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/robots.txt\n# Content-Type must be text/plain (not text/html)\n\ncurl -s https://findmyinvite.com/robots.txt | head\n# first line: User-agent:\n\ncurl -sI https://findmyinvite.com/sitemap.xml\n# Content-Type must be application/xml or text/xml (not text/html)\n\ncurl -s https://findmyinvite.com/sitemap.xml | head\n# starts with <?xml or <urlset\n```\n\nPreview deploys on this branch can use the same curls against the Vercel preview URL (not `*.vercel.app` in the sitemap body — only the live apex host is listed).\n\n## Tests\n\n`tests/server-share.mjs` now asserts the catch-all rewrite leaves `/robots.txt` and `/sitemap.xml` unmatched (so they are not rewritten to `/index.html`). That assertion passed locally. Other server tests that import `@vercel/blob` failed in this environment because `node_modules` is incomplete; that is unrelated to this change.\n<!-- CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_END -->\n\n<div><a CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\n## Why\nEpic 2 gap-fill for live `/akay/shortlist`. List, filters, and cards already exist. Approve/Reject were easy to miss under the tall page preview, and approve+queue was two REST writes instead of one transaction.\n\n## What\n- **G1** Approve and Reject stay on every card; disabled when the row is not `proposed`\n- **G2** Bulk bar still Approve/Reject selected, max 50, per-id ok/fail\n- **G3/G5** Privileged RPC `approve_shortlist_candidate`: `status=approved`, bump `updated_at`, insert `replication_queue` (`queued`, empty assignee) `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` in one transaction. Re-approve → 409, queue count unchanged\n- **G4** `reject_shortlist_candidate` sets `rejected` only and never touches the queue\n- **G6–G8** Drawer keeps full reason, competitor/catalogue links, Open template, replication status after approve; empty-state copy for Approved/Rejected/filter miss; cropped card preview so actions stay above the fold\n\nDoes **not** recreate list/filters, Upcoming→Shortlist promote, Scout mining, or Pipeline/Templates.\n\n## Verification\n- `tests/server-akay-shortlist.mjs` pass: approve enqueues once, re-approve 409, reject never touches `replication_queue`, bulk >50 is 400\n- RPC smoke on prod: missing id returns `{ok:false,error:not_found}` with no writes\n- Production-like preview: `/akay` gate + `/akay/shortlist` mount (local env has no Supabase, so list shows the expected “Publishing is not configured yet.” banner)\n- Card layout checked at mobile/desktop with the same CSS: Approve / Reject / Open template stay on the card above the fold; bulk bar on select; drawer footer actions; non-proposed buttons disabled\n\n## QA (after merge)\n1. Proposed → Approve one Batch A card → Approved = +1 and `replication_queue` 0→1 (`queued`)\n2. Same id Approve again → error / no second queue row\n3. Another Proposed → Reject → Rejected +1; queue unchanged\n4. Select 3 Proposed → bulk Approve → +3 queue rows\n5. Non-proposed: Approve/Reject and checkbox disabled\n\nDo not treat the 3 already-rejected video templates as this QA. Live DB at implementation: 22 proposed / 3 rejected / 0 approved / queue 0.\n\nRPC is already applied on prod Supabase `qqvcptjkfcjkwbkookcm` (`service_role` execute only). This PR is the operator UI + API caller. `/akay`: **Traffic**, **Journeys**, **Live**, **Shortlist** — each is its own page, sized for iPhone.\n- Shortlist: status chips with counts, tier/competitor/batch/direct/search filters, card list
